    public static class SwimEval
    {
        // ---- fixed simulation constants (no Time.*, no tunables read from the sim) ----
        public const float Dt = 1f / 120f;          // fixed integration timestep (s)
        const float RhoWater = 1000f;               // water density (kg/m^3) -- buoyancy + added mass
        // Structural-mass unit fix (2026-07-02): Reconstruct builds segment mass from the abstract DENSITY GENE
        // (neutral 4), but the added-mass tensor + all forces use SI RhoWater=1000, so structural inertia entered the
        // EoM ~250x too light (added-mass-dominated -> the body integrated too light in transients). The gene scale is
        // linear (gene_density * KgPerSimMass = real kg/m^3), so real SI mass = gene-mass * KgPerSimMass, a UNIVERSAL
        // factor. Applied ONLY at the inertia terms (m00/m11/m22, iYaw structural), NOT at comBF (a mass RATIO -> the
        // scale cancels) or COT (keeps sim-mass so density cancels in COT*mBody). LOCAL const so SwimEval stays
        // standalone (header HARD CONSTRAINT); RhoNeutral mirrors the BodyGraph density-gene neutral / SimUnits.
        const float RhoNeutral = 4f;
        const float KgPerSimMass = RhoWater / RhoNeutral;   // = 250 (matches SimUnits.KgPerSimMass)

        // ---- geometry caps + clamps, MIRRORED from SwimmerBuilder (physics build, NOT the 28/6 measure caps) ----
        const int MaxParts = 16;                    // SwimmerBuilder.MaxParts
        const int MaxDepth = 5;                     // SwimmerBuilder.MaxDepth
        const float SurfaceThickness = 0.1f;        // SwimmerBuilder.SurfaceThickness
        const float MinX = 0.12f, MinY = 0.12f, MinZ = 0.3f;   // SwimmerBuilder.cs:106-107 clamps

        // ---- gait amplitude cap, MIRRORED from SwimmerBuilder.cs:133 ----
        const float AmpMax = 58f;   // physical joint-flex cap (deg). Amplitude FLOOR REMOVED 2026-07-02 (was
                                    // clamp(jAmp+20, 32, 58)): the +20 bias + 32deg floor forced every body to thrash
                                    // regardless of genes, blocking efficient-gait evolution. jAmp now maps DIRECTLY to
                                    // ampDeg so amplitude + head->tail taper can evolve under the loco cost (form-is-function).

        // Task 18d (byte-neutral behind ellipMassGain): segment INERTIAL mass model. At gain 0 -> the box mass
        // (scx*scy*scz*density, == live rb.mass) EXACTLY. Ramped to 1, scale by pi/6 so the SAME segment is an
        // ELLIPSOID for BOTH inertia and hydrodynamics (volEllipsoid already uses pi/6), removing the pi/6~=0.5236
        // box-vs-ellipsoid mismatch. SwimEval is a standalone file, so the knob is an internal const here.
        internal const float ellipMassGain = 0f;
        internal static float SegmentMass(float boxMass, float gain) => boxMass * Mathf.Lerp(1f, Mathf.PI / 6f, gain);

        /// One reconstructed body part: a scaled unit sphere = ellipsoid, with its rest pose in body frame,
        /// its ellipsoid volume, its box inertial mass (matching live rb.mass), and its diagonal added-mass
        /// tensor (Lamb k-factors * rho * V). Pure data -- no Unity object behind it.
        public struct Segment
        {
            public Vector3 restPos;       // body-frame rest CENTER (== BuildPart wp at bodyScale=1)
            public Quaternion restRot;    // body-frame rest ORIENTATION (== BuildPart wr); local +z is the long axis
            public Vector3 localPos;      // attach offset relative to the parent (BuildPart localPos)
            public Quaternion localRot;   // rest local rotation relative to the parent (BuildPart localRot)
            public float a, b, c;         // semi-axes: a = sc.x/2 (half-width, lateral), b = sc.y/2, c = sc.z/2 (half-length)
            public float volume;          // ELLIPSOID displaced volume (pi/6)*sc.x*sc.y*sc.z -> buoyancy + added mass
            public float mass;            // BOX inertial mass max(0.1, sc.x*sc.y*sc.z*density) (matches live rb.mass)
            public float areaX, areaY, areaZ;   // anisotropic drag reference areas (local): X=sc.y*sc.z, Y=sc.x*sc.z, Z=sc.x*sc.y
            public float maX, maY, maZ;   // added-mass tensor diagonal (body frame): k_i * rho * V
            public float finMaPerp;       // Surface fins: the broadside (perpendicular-to-broad-face) added-mass magnitude, retained for the orientation-projected reactive thrust. 0 for Segments.
            public int parentIndex;       // -1 for the root
            public int depth;             // graph depth (root = 0)
            public float side;            // +1 / -1 mirror side
            public float ampDeg;          // gait amplitude (deg) of THIS part's joint; 0 if it has no joint (root)
            public float phase;           // gait phase = -depth * swimWave (rad)
            public bool hasJoint;         // parentIndex >= 0 -> carries a hinge actuator (live: parentRb != null)
            public bool isTail;           // the trailing edge (posterior-most segment, largest +z reach)
            public PartGene gene;         // the source part gene (render/skin metadata: size, type); no float coupling
        }

        // Threaded through the reconstruction DFS (mirrors SwimmerBuilder.Ctx, data-only).
        /// (the fixed summation order) and the index of the trailing-edge segment. bodyScale is 1
        /// (matches the live ColonyAvatars build seam). NO GameObjects, NO PhysX.
        internal static List<Segment> Reconstruct(BodyGenome g, out int tailIndex, bool withAddedMass = true, float finPlaneGain = 0f)
        {
            var ctx = new Ctx { parts = 0, swimWave = g.swimWave, withAddedMass = withAddedMass, finPlaneGain = finPlaneGain };
            BuildPart(g.root, -1, 1f, 0, ctx, Vector3.zero, Quaternion.identity);

            // Trailing edge = the posterior-most segment (largest +z reach in body frame). Ties are broken
            // by DFS index (the later-built one wins), so it is a fully deterministic pure function of the
            // genome -- mirrors "builds[] last element along the deepest tail chain" (v2 design 1.3).
            tailIndex = 0;
            for (int i = 1; i < ctx.segs.Count; i++)
                if (ctx.segs[i].restPos.z >= ctx.segs[tailIndex].restPos.z) tailIndex = i;

            if (ctx.segs.Count > 0)
            {
                var t = ctx.segs[tailIndex];
                t.isTail = true;
                ctx.segs[tailIndex] = t;
            }
            return ctx.segs;
        }

        /// Pure DFS that mirrors SwimmerBuilder.BuildPart (SwimmerBuilder.cs:93-141) EXACTLY in recursion
        /// shape, order, caps, transform algebra and physics clamps -- but emits ONLY data.
        static void BuildPart(PartGene p, int parentIndex, float side, int depth, Ctx ctx, Vector3 worldPos, Quaternion worldRot)
        {
            if (ctx.parts >= MaxParts || depth > MaxDepth) return;   // SwimmerBuilder.cs:95
            ctx.parts++;

            // --- transform math (SwimmerBuilder.cs:98-101), bodyScale = 1 ---
            Quaternion localRot = Quaternion.Euler(p.orient.x, p.orient.y * side, p.orient.z * side);
            Vector3 localPos = new Vector3(p.attach.x * side, p.attach.y, p.attach.z);
            Quaternion wr = worldRot * localRot;
            Vector3 wp = worldPos + worldRot * localPos;

            // --- physics clamps (SwimmerBuilder.cs:106-107) ---
            float ey = (p.type == PartType.Surface) ? SurfaceThickness : Mathf.Max(MinY, p.size.y);
            float scx = Mathf.Max(MinX, p.size.x);
            float scy = ey;
            float scz = Mathf.Max(MinZ, p.size.z);

            float a = scx * 0.5f, b = scy * 0.5f, c = scz * 0.5f;
            float volEllipsoid = (Mathf.PI / 6f) * scx * scy * scz;             // (pi/6)(2a)(2b)(2c)
            float massBox = SegmentMass(Mathf.Max(0.1f, scx * scy * scz * p.density), ellipMassGain);   // live rb.mass (SwimmerBuilder.cs:116); Task 18d: ellipMassGain=0 -> box mass (byte-identical)

            // anisotropic drag reference areas (local frame): area perpendicular to each local axis.
            float areaX = scy * scz;   // lateral (broadside on local x)
            float areaY = scx * scz;   // vertical
            float areaZ = scx * scy;   // frontal (along local +z, the long axis)

            // added-mass tensor diagonal (body frame) via Lamb k-factors of the axis ratios.
            // Skip on the BodyMass path (withAddedMass=false): maX/maY/maZ left at 0 (struct default).
            // CenterOfMass/BodyMass only read segment.mass and .restPos, so the zeroed tensor is never used.
            float maX = 0f, maY = 0f, maZ = 0f, finMaPerp = 0f;
            if (ctx.withAddedMass)
            {
                LambK(a, b, c, out float kx, out float ky, out float kz);
                maY = ky * RhoWater * volEllipsoid;
                maZ = kz * RhoWater * volEllipsoid;
                if (p.type == PartType.Surface)
                {
                    // m_perp = the fin's broadside (perpendicular-to-broad-face) added mass, using the TRUE span (Lamb 1932).
                    float spanY = Mathf.Max(MinY, p.size.y);
                    LambK(a, spanY * 0.5f, c, out float kxSpan, out _, out _);
                    float mPerp = kxSpan * RhoWater * (Mathf.PI / 6f) * scx * spanY * scz;
                    // Byte-neutral gate: at finPlaneGain=0 the broadside mass sits on the in-plane local-X (today's restore) and
                    // maY stays thin; at 1 it moves onto the local-Y NORMAL where a thin plate physically carries it, and the
                    // existing M_eff = R·diag·Rᵀ rotation then makes it orientation-correct for any roll.
                    float maXInplane = kx * RhoWater * volEllipsoid;   // the natural (small) in-plane value, as for a Segment
                    maX = Mathf.Lerp(mPerp, maXInplane, ctx.finPlaneGain);
                    maY = Mathf.Lerp(maY,   mPerp,      ctx.finPlaneGain);  // maY currently holds the thin-plate value from above
                    finMaPerp = mPerp;                                     // retained for the reactive-thrust projection
                }
                else
                {
                    maX = kx * RhoWater * volEllipsoid;   // Segment: bit-identical to the original single-LambK path
                }
            }

            bool hasJoint = parentIndex >= 0;   // live: only non-root parts (parentRb != null) get an actuator
            float ampDeg = hasJoint ? Mathf.Clamp(p.jAmp, 0f, AmpMax) : 0f;   // jAmp -> deg DIRECTLY (no +20 bias, no 32deg floor): a genome can now express a gentle efficient cruise (low jAmp) OR a thrash (high jAmp)
            float phase = -depth * ctx.swimWave;

            ctx.segs.Add(new Segment
            {
                restPos = wp,
                restRot = wr,
                localPos = localPos,
                localRot = localRot,
                a = a, b = b, c = c,
                volume = volEllipsoid,
                mass = massBox,
                areaX = areaX, areaY = areaY, areaZ = areaZ,
                maX = maX, maY = maY, maZ = maZ,
                finMaPerp = finMaPerp,
                parentIndex = parentIndex,
                depth = depth,
                side = side,
                ampDeg = ampDeg,
                phase = phase,
                hasJoint = hasJoint,
                isTail = false,
                gene = p,
            });
            int myIndex = ctx.segs.Count - 1;
        // CDi = CL^2/(pi*e*AR) make ASPECT RATIO the efficiency lever; the fin is FEATHERED by the tail slope s
        // so it holds a near-optimal angle of attack across the stroke. Pure + deterministic + allocation-free.
        internal const float FinProfileCd = 0.02f;   // thin-foil profile/skin-friction drag coefficient
        internal const float FinSpanEff   = 0.9f;    // Oswald span efficiency (~0.9 for near-elliptical loading)
        internal const float FinStallAoA  = 0.35f;   // ~20 deg attached-flow AoA limit (dynamic-stall bound)

        /// Foil-plane alignment: |n̂_fin · n̂_heave|. The plate is thin in local +Y, so its normal is
        /// restRot·up; lateralUnit is the swim-plane lateral heave direction. 0 = edge-on (no circulatory
        /// lift), 1 = broadside (full lift). Pure + deterministic. (Garrick 1936: circulatory lift comes from
        /// the flow component NORMAL to the foil; only heave along n̂_fin creates angle of attack.)
        public static float FinAlign(Quaternion restRot, Vector3 lateralUnit)
            => Mathf.Abs(Vector3.Dot(restRot * Vector3.up, lateralUnit.normalized));

        /// Reactive-channel orientation scale: the fin's added mass along the lateral heave is m_perp·align²
        /// (flat-plate added-mass tensor contracted twice with the motion direction; Lamb 1932). This returns the
        /// gated factor Lerp(1, align², finPlaneGain): 1 at gain 0 (byte-neutral), align² at gain 1 (0 edge-on, 1 broadside).
        public static float FinReactiveScaleOf(float align, float finPlaneGain)
            => Mathf.Lerp(1f, align * align, finPlaneGain);

        /// Circulatory foil thrust [N] + its induced/profile wake loss pWake [W]. aL = finite-wing lift slope
        /// (1/rad), AR = span/chord, S = planform area [m^2]; U = forward speed, Vt = lateral heave speed,
        /// s = tail slope (feathering pitch = dh/dx). Energy closes EXACTLY: P_in = T*U + pWake (see companion).
        public static float CirculatoryLiftThrust(float aL, float AR, float S, float U, float Vt, float s, out float pWake)
        {
            pWake = 0f;
            float Ucl = Mathf.Max(0f, U);                        // no circulatory lift travelling backward
            float Q2 = Ucl * Ucl + Vt * Vt;
            if (Q2 < 1e-8f) return 0f;                           // truly still -> no throughflow -> no lift
            float Q = Mathf.Sqrt(Q2);
            float pitch = Mathf.Asin(Mathf.Clamp(s, -1f, 1f));   // fin geometric pitch from the backbone slope
            float alpha = Mathf.Clamp(Mathf.Atan2(Vt, Ucl) - pitch, -FinStallAoA, FinStallAoA);   // feathered AoA
            float CL  = aL * alpha;
            float q   = 0.5f * RhoWater * Ucl * Ucl * S;         // KJ dynamic pressure on the FORWARD throughflow
            float L   = q * CL;
            float CDi = FinProfileCd + (CL * CL) / (Mathf.PI * FinSpanEff * Mathf.Max(AR, 1e-4f));
            float D   = q * CDi;
            float sinB = Vt / Q, cosB = Ucl / Q;
            pWake = D * Q;                                       // shed downwash + profile loss (>= 0)
            return L * sinB - D * cosB;                          // forward: lift tilt minus induced-drag retardation
        }

        /// Energy/test companion: the muscle input power for the same inputs. Identically P_in = T*U + pWake
        /// (lift does zero net work; the muscle heaves against the lateral fluid reaction F_lat).
        public static float CirculatoryInputPower(float aL, float AR, float S, float U, float Vt, float s)
        {
            float Ucl = Mathf.Max(0f, U);
            float Q2 = Ucl * Ucl + Vt * Vt;
            if (Q2 < 1e-8f) return 0f;
            float Q = Mathf.Sqrt(Q2);
            float pitch = Mathf.Asin(Mathf.Clamp(s, -1f, 1f));
            float alpha = Mathf.Clamp(Mathf.Atan2(Vt, Ucl) - pitch, -FinStallAoA, FinStallAoA);
            float CL = aL * alpha;
            float q  = 0.5f * RhoWater * Ucl * Ucl * S;
            float L  = q * CL, D = q * (FinProfileCd + (CL * CL) / (Mathf.PI * FinSpanEff * Mathf.Max(AR, 1e-4f)));
            float sinB = Vt / Q, cosB = Ucl / Q;
            float Fn = L * cosB + D * sinB;                      // lateral fluid reaction the muscle heaves against

            // AmpMax-safe turn-bias cap (Task 7): keeps the biased peak posed angle (ampDeg_j + turnCmd*depth_j) within
            // the evolved gait clamp AmpMax for every jointed segment. Safe single bound: maxAmpDeg + turnCmd*maxDepth
            // <= AmpMax. A fully-maxed gait (maxAmpDeg == AmpMax) yields 0 authority; a normal gait retains a positive cap.
            static float DeriveMaxTurnCmd(List<Segment> segs)
            {
                float maxAmp = 0f; int maxDepth = 0;
                for (int j = 0; j < segs.Count; j++)
                    if (segs[j].hasJoint)
                    {
                        if (segs[j].ampDeg > maxAmp) maxAmp = segs[j].ampDeg;
                        if (segs[j].depth > maxDepth) maxDepth = segs[j].depth;
                    }
                if (maxDepth <= 0) return 0f;                               // no biasable jointed segment
                return Mathf.Max(0f, (AmpMax - maxAmp) / maxDepth);        // AmpMax = 58 (SwimmerBuilder clamp)
            }

            public Vector3 Velocity => _vCom;

            // Forward kinematics: pose every segment in the body frame at time `time`, applying each
            // joint's gait angle about its local up. Parents precede children in DFS order, so a single
            // forward pass suffices. (No GameObjects -- pure quaternion algebra.)
            void Pose(float time)
            {
                float w = 2f * Mathf.PI * _swimFreq;
                for (int j = 0; j < _segs.Count; j++)
                {
                    var sg = _segs[j];
                    Vector3 pPos; Quaternion pRot;
                    if (sg.parentIndex < 0) { pPos = Vector3.zero; pRot = Quaternion.identity; }
                    else { pPos = _pos[sg.parentIndex]; pRot = _rot[sg.parentIndex]; }

                    Quaternion localFlexed = sg.localRot;
                    if (sg.hasJoint)
                    {
                        float theta = sg.ampDeg * Mathf.Sin(w * time + sg.phase);   // degrees
                        // LIVE turning bias (spec §4.1): a progressive DC body bend, head->tail, that makes
                        // the evolved gait asymmetric so the EBT force law produces a net yaw torque (Task 3).
                        // GUARDED on `_turnCmd != 0f` so the one-shot measurement path -- where `_turnCmd` is
                        // always the default 0 -- executes the ORIGINAL expression verbatim (no `+0f` add, hence
                        // no -0.0f/+0.0f sign-of-zero drift) => Pose is BIT-IDENTICAL there. On the live path
                        // (`_turnCmd != 0`) this adds `turnCmd*depth` degrees to each jointed segment.
                        if (_turnCmd != 0f) theta += _turnCmd * sg.depth;
                        localFlexed = sg.localRot * Quaternion.AngleAxis(theta, Vector3.up);
                    }
                    _rot[j] = pRot * localFlexed;
                    _pos[j] = pPos + pRot * sg.localPos;
                }
            }

            Vector3 ComWorld()
            {
                Vector3 c = Vector3.zero;
                for (int j = 0; j < _segs.Count; j++) c += _segs[j].mass * _pos[j];
                return _xCom + (_mBody > 1e-6f ? c / _mBody : Vector3.zero);
            }

            // The COM in world space. On the LIVE path `_xCom` IS the COM (COM-direct, §11.3), so ComNow
            // returns it directly. On the one-shot path `_live` is false and this returns the ORIGINAL
            // ComWorld() (where `_xCom` is the body origin) -> the measurement path is unchanged.
            internal Vector3 ComNow() => _live ? _xCom : ComWorld();

            internal Quaternion OrientationNow() => _orientation;

            // Yaw angular velocity (rad/s) accessor -- the §11.1 guard test asserts |OmegaNow()| never reaches
            // MaxOmega during a normal turn (drag-set, not clamp-set). Read-only view of _omegaY.
            internal float OmegaNow() => _omegaY;

            // Task 7 test accessor: the derived AmpMax-safe turn-bias cap for this body.
            internal float MaxTurnCmd => _maxTurnCmd;
            internal static float MaxOmegaBackstop => MaxOmega;   // [EBT] instrumentation: the §11.1 numerical yaw-rate cap

            // Segment count (== reconstructed parts). Render/skin bone count.
            internal int SegCount => _pos.Length;

            // Fill the per-segment WORLD pose for the LIVE path (COM-direct), Unity-light (writes caller arrays).
            // World pos = COM + orientation*(bodyFrameSegPos - bodyFrameCom); world rot = orientation*bodyFrameSegRot.
            // Reads the current gait pose (set by the last Pose(_t) in StepLive). Render-only accessor.
            internal void FillSegmentPoses(Vector3[] pos, Quaternion[] rot)
            {
                Vector3 comBF = Vector3.zero;
                for (int j = 0; j < _segs.Count; j++) comBF += _segs[j].mass * _pos[j];
                comBF = _mBody > 1e-6f ? comBF / _mBody : Vector3.zero;
                for (int j = 0; j < _pos.Length; j++)
                {
                    pos[j] = _xCom + _orientation * (_pos[j] - comBF);
                    rot[j] = _orientation * _rot[j];
                }
            }

            // Tail TIP world point in the body frame (excludes _xCom): the tail CENTER plus the tail's OWN
            // flexed +z axis times its half-length c. Unlike _pos[_tail] (the center), the tip MOVES with the
            // tail's own wag: Pose folds a segment's own joint rotation into _rot[j] but NOT into _pos[j]
            // (_pos[j] = pPos + pRot*localPos uses only the PARENT chain). Sampling U, V_t AND the slope s all
            // at this single tip keeps Lighthill's T = 1/4 m_t a^2 k^2 (c^2 - U^2) self-consistent (the EBT
            // derivation assumes trailing-EDGE/tip evaluation, not segment-center).
            Vector3 TailTip()
                => _segs.Count > 0 ? _pos[_tail] + _rot[_tail] * Vector3.forward * _segs[_tail].c : Vector3.zero;

            // One fixed semi-implicit step. `measuring` gates impulse/work accumulation.
            public void Step(bool measuring)
            {
                _t += Dt;
                for (int i = 0; i < _pos.Length; i++) _prevPos[i] = _pos[i];
                Vector3 prevTip = TailTip();                             // tail TIP at the PREVIOUS pose (pre-Pose arrays)
                Pose(_t);

                // --- trailing-edge reactive thrust (the only thrust term), sampled at the tail TIP ---
                Vector3 tipNow = TailTip();                             // tail TIP at the new pose
                Vector3 uTail = _vCom + (tipNow - prevTip) / Dt;        // tail-TIP world velocity (captures the tail's own wag)
                float U = Vector3.Dot(uTail, _fThrust);
                float Vt = Vector3.Dot(uTail, _nThrust);
                Vector3 tHat = _rot[_tail] * Vector3.forward;            // tail backbone (local +z) in world
                float s = Vector3.Dot(tHat, _nThrust);
                float tReact = ReactiveThrust(_mt, Vt, U, s);
                float Wt = Crossflow(Vt, U, s);
                float pWake = WakePower(_mt, Mathf.Max(0f, U), Wt);

                // Circulatory foil channel (Garrick's other half; ONLY a Surface caudal fin). The reactive term
                // above already carries the fin's ADDED-MASS half via its restored m_t (self-start); this adds the
                // bound-circulation LIFT (efficiency at speed). Distinct physics -> additive, not a double-count.
                float tFin = 0f, pFin = 0f;
                if (_finActive)
                {
                    float vtEff = Vt * Mathf.Lerp(1f, _finAlign, _finPlaneGain);   // Garrick lift ∝ heave·normal; = Vt at gain 0
                    tFin = CirculatoryLiftThrust(_finAL, _finAR, _finS, U, vtEff, s, out pFin);
                }

                // --- AXIAL-ONLY form drag over ALL segments (dissipative) + effective mass matrix ---
                Vector3 fDrag = Vector3.zero;
                float wDrag = 0f;
                float m00 = _mBody * KgPerSimMass, m01 = 0f, m02 = 0f, m11 = _mBody * KgPerSimMass, m12 = 0f, m22 = _mBody * KgPerSimMass;   // structural inertia in SI kg (gene-mass * KgPerSimMass) -> matches the SI added-mass tensor below
                float half = 0.5f * RhoWater * _cd;
                for (int j = 0; j < _segs.Count; j++)
                {
                    var sg = _segs[j];
                    Quaternion R = _rot[j];
                    Vector3 uj = _vCom + (_pos[j] - _prevPos[j]) / Dt;
                    Vector3 vloc = Quaternion.Inverse(R) * uj;            // segment velocity in local frame
                    // Charge form drag ONLY on the streamwise local +z (frontal area areaZ) -- genuine
                    // skin/form friction. The LATERAL broadside terms (areaX width, areaY height) are
                    // deliberately NOT charged: a slender undulator's lateral resistance is REACTIVE
                    // (added mass), already modeled by the EBT m_t / pWake / tReact reaction below;
                    // charging a v^2 form drag on the same lateral tail-wag double-counts that momentum
                    // and blows up as the 2-5 m/s wag velocity squared. (vloc.x/vloc.y intentionally unused.)
                    Vector3 floc = new Vector3(
                        0f,
                        0f,
                        -half * sg.areaZ * Mathf.Abs(vloc.z) * vloc.z);
                    Vector3 fworld = R * floc;
                    fDrag += fworld;
                    wDrag += Mathf.Max(0f, -Vector3.Dot(fworld, uj));     // dissipated power (>= 0)

                    // M_eff += R * diag(maX,maY,maZ) * R^T  (added-mass tensor in world frame)
                    Vector3 cx = R * Vector3.right, cy = R * Vector3.up, cz = R * Vector3.forward;
                    float ax = sg.maX, ay = sg.maY, az = sg.maZ;
                    m00 += ax * cx.x * cx.x + ay * cy.x * cy.x + az * cz.x * cz.x;
                    m01 += ax * cx.x * cx.y + ay * cy.x * cy.y + az * cz.x * cz.y;
                    m02 += ax * cx.x * cx.z + ay * cy.x * cy.z + az * cz.x * cz.z;
                    m11 += ax * cx.y * cx.y + ay * cy.y * cy.y + az * cz.y * cz.y;
                    m12 += ax * cx.y * cx.z + ay * cy.y * cy.z + az * cz.y * cz.z;
                    m22 += ax * cx.z * cx.z + ay * cy.z * cy.z + az * cz.z * cz.z;
                }

                Vector3 fStream = (tReact + tFin) * _fThrust + fDrag;    // reactive + circulatory foil; NO buoyancy: net vertical zeroed
                Vector3 dv = SolveSym3(m00, m01, m02, m11, m12, m22, fStream * Dt);
                _vCom += dv;
                _vCom.y = 0f;                                            // constrain to the horizontal swim plane
                _xCom += _vCom * Dt;

                if (measuring)
                {
                    ReactiveImpulse += Mathf.Abs(tReact) * Dt;
                    DragImpulse += Mathf.Abs(Vector3.Dot(fDrag, _fThrust)) * Dt;
                    MechWork += (pWake + pFin + wDrag) * Dt;            // + fin induced/profile wake loss (energy closes: P_in = T*U + pWake)
                    ForwardThrustImpulse += (tReact + tFin) * Dt;        // SIGNED; forward momentum both channels feed the COM (= shed rearward to the wake)
                }
            }

            public void Run(int steps, bool measuring) { for (int i = 0; i < steps; i++) Step(measuring); }

            // Test hook (spec §4.2 seam): set the latched gait-bias directly (Task 4 replaces this with the
            // LatchTurnCmd P-controller). Non-zero => the Pose(+turnCmd*depth) bias engages and the emergent
            // yaw torque appears. INERT to the one-shot path (which never calls it).
            internal void SetTurnCmd(float c) => _turnCmd = c;

        {
            if (!IsFinite(p.cruiseSpeed)) p.cruiseSpeed = 0f;
            if (!IsFinite(p.costOfTransport)) p.costOfTransport = 0f;
            if (!IsFinite(p.reactiveRatio)) p.reactiveRatio = 0f;
            return p;
        }

        static bool IsFinite(float f) => !float.IsNaN(f) && !float.IsInfinity(f);
        static bool IsFinite(Vector3 v) => IsFinite(v.x) && IsFinite(v.y) && IsFinite(v.z);

        /// Solve M * x = rhs for a symmetric positive-definite 3x3 M = [[m00,m01,m02],[m01,m11,m12],[m02,m12,m22]].
        static Vector3 SolveSym3(float m00, float m01, float m02, float m11, float m12, float m22, Vector3 rhs)
        {
            float c00 = m11 * m22 - m12 * m12;
            float c01 = m02 * m12 - m01 * m22;
            float c02 = m01 * m12 - m02 * m11;
            float det = m00 * c00 + m01 * c01 + m02 * c02;
            if (Mathf.Abs(det) < 1e-12f) return new Vector3(rhs.x / Mathf.Max(m00, 1e-6f), 0f, rhs.z / Mathf.Max(m22, 1e-6f));
            float c11 = m00 * m22 - m02 * m02;
            float c12 = m02 * m01 - m00 * m12;
            float c22 = m00 * m11 - m01 * m01;
            float inv = 1f / det;
            return new Vector3(
                (c00 * rhs.x + c01 * rhs.y + c02 * rhs.z) * inv,
                (c01 * rhs.x + c11 * rhs.y + c12 * rhs.z) * inv,
                (c02 * rhs.x + c12 * rhs.y + c22 * rhs.z) * inv);
        }

        // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
        // TEST SEAMS (Task 1) -- pure, allocation-light entry points for the EditMode litmus.
        // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// Reconstruct the geometry for tests. Returns the DFS-ordered segments and the trailing-edge index.
        public static Segment[] ReconstructForTest(BodyGenome g, out int tailIndex)
        {
            var segs = Reconstruct(g, out tailIndex);

        // All thrust is a SINGLE boundary term: the lateral fluid-momentum flux shed at the trailing
        // edge. It is NOT a body-interior volume integral (which would telescope to ~0 for a steady
        // traveling wave). At the tail, with forward speed U along the heading, lateral tail velocity
        // V_t (= dh/dt), and tail-backbone slope s (= dh/dx), the crossflow shed into the wake is
        //
        //     W_t = V_t + U * s            (= dh/dt + U dh/dx)
        //
        // and the standard EBT bookkeeping (Lighthill 1960/1971) gives, per unit trailing length m_t:
        //
        //     T_react = m_t * ( V_t*W_t - 0.5*W_t^2 )          (reactive thrust)
        //     P_in    = m_t * U * ( V_t*W_t )                   (rate body works on the fluid)
        //     P_wake  = 0.5 * m_t * U * W_t^2                   (irrecoverable shed wake KE)
        //
        // The thrust term ALGEBRAICALLY reduces (substitute W_t = V_t + U s, expand) to the classic
        // Lighthill form, which is what ReactiveThrust evaluates:
        //
        //     T_react = 0.5 * m_t * ( V_t^2 - U^2 * s^2 )
        //
        // Consequences that EMERGE (no penalty terms anywhere):
        //   * Traveling wave h = a sin(kx - wt): <T_react> = 1/4 m_t a^2 k^2 (c^2 - U^2) > 0 for U<c,
        //     -> 0 as U->c (the famous EBT result; verified in the litmus).
        //   * No lateral motion (V_t=0) on a straight tail (s=0) -> T_react = 0 (rigid body, L1).
        //   * A standing/incoherent gait nets ~0 thrust at its operating speed (V_t^2 = U^2 s^2) yet
        //     still sheds wake KE (W_t != 0) -> cost-of-transport -> infinity (L2). A tail-wag IS
        //     propulsive (sculling); coherence shows up as EFFICIENCY (lower wake for equal thrust),
        //     not as a thrust penalty.
        //   * Energy closes EXACTLY, instantaneously: P_in == T_react*U + P_wake.

        /// Crossflow shed at the trailing edge: W_t = V_t + U*s = dh/dt + U dh/dx.
        public static float Crossflow(float Vt, float U, float s) => Vt + U * s;

        /// Reactive (thrust) boundary term, Lighthill reduced form: T_react = 0.5 m_t (V_t^2 - U^2 s^2).
        /// (Identically equal to the raw flux form m_t (V_t*W_t - 0.5 W_t^2); see ReactiveThrustFlux.)
        public static float ReactiveThrust(float mt, float Vt, float U, float s)
            => 0.5f * mt * (Vt * Vt - U * U * s * s);

        /// The same reactive thrust written as the raw shed-momentum flux m_t (V_t*W_t - 0.5 W_t^2).
        /// Provided so a test can confirm the two forms agree (the algebraic reduction holds numerically).
        public static float ReactiveThrustFlux(float mt, float Vt, float U, float s)
        {
            float Wt = Crossflow(Vt, U, s);
            return mt * (Vt * Wt - 0.5f * Wt * Wt);
        }

        /// Irrecoverable wake kinetic energy shed per unit time: P_wake = 0.5 m_t U W_t^2 (>= 0 for U>=0).
        public static float WakePower(float mt, float U, float Wt) => 0.5f * mt * U * Wt * Wt;

        /// Rate the body does work on the fluid: P_in = m_t U V_t W_t. Closes energy: P_in = T_react U + P_wake.
        public static float InputPower(float mt, float U, float Vt, float Wt) => mt * U * Vt * Wt;

        /// Transverse (lateral) added mass PER UNIT LENGTH at the tail: m_t = m_a_lateral / (2c).
        /// The lateral crossflow acts along the tail's local x (width); 2c is the tail length (local +z).
        internal static float TailMassPerLength(in Segment tail)
            => tail.maX / Mathf.Max(2f * tail.c, 1e-4f);

        // --- Circulatory (finite-wing foil) thrust: Garrick's OTHER half, for a caudal FIN (Surface tail) ---
            Assert.That(Mathf.Abs(meanT), Is.LessThan(1e-3f), "standing wave nets ~0 thrust at its operating speed (EMERGES)");
            Assert.That(meanWake, Is.GreaterThan(0.01f), "yet it keeps dumping KE into the wake -> COT -> infinity");
            Assert.That(meanWakeCoherent, Is.LessThan(0.05f * meanWake), "the coherent wave feathers (tiny wake) at the same point");
        }

        [Test]
        public void Energy_closes_exactly_P_in_equals_thrust_work_plus_wake()
        {
            // Instantaneous, exact: P_in = T_react*U + P_wake for arbitrary kinematics.
            (float mt, float Vt, float U, float s)[] cases =
            {
                (2f, 0.3f, 0.5f, 0.2f), (1.7f, -0.25f, 1.1f, 0.33f), (0.8f, 0.6f, 0.2f, -0.5f),
            };
            foreach (var (mt, Vt, U, s) in cases)
            {
                float Wt = SwimEval.Crossflow(Vt, U, s);
                float lhs = SwimEval.InputPower(mt, U, Vt, Wt);
                float rhs = SwimEval.ReactiveThrust(mt, Vt, U, s) * U + SwimEval.WakePower(mt, U, Wt);
                Assert.That(lhs, Is.EqualTo(rhs).Within(1e-5f), "P_in = T_react*U + P_wake (term-by-term)");
            }
        }

   183	
   184	```python
   185	# core/config.py
   186	@dataclass(frozen=True)
   187	class Config: device="cpu"; dtype_hot="float32"; n_cap=1024; w=1; dt=1/120; world=WorldConfig()

   190	@dataclass

   192	    def advance(self, dt): self.now += dt; self.step += 1; self.dt = dt
   193	
   194	# core/colony.py — Gymnasium-shaped; step is a pure fn of (prior state, dt, action); torch.inference_mode()
   195	class Colony:
   196	    def reset(self, seed:int)->dict          # seed_everything; SimClock→0; ledger.reset(reservoirs); obs
   197	    def step(self, dt:float, action:dict)->dict   # S0: NO-OP integrator; ledger.assert_closed(tol=1e-9)
   198	    def state(self)->ColonyState ; def load(self, s)->None

   204	
   205	# physics/force.py
   206	class ForceContributor(Protocol):
   207	    def accumulate(self, body:DevelopedBody, pose, vel, medium:MediumSample)->ForceTorquePower: ...
   208

   215	def solve_sym3(m00,m01,m02,m11,m12,m22, rhs)->Tensor["B,3"]   # donor cofactor op-order EXACTLY
   216	def segment_index_add(vals, body_id, B)->Tensor  # deterministic; precomputed unique slots; NEVER scatter_add_

   238	### 1.9 CI pipeline (job order encodes priority)

   240	`.github/workflows/ci.yml`, CPU-only runner (CUDA determinism validated on dev box, reported not gated cross-machine, §7.1.2). Order: **`boundary` (import-linter + ruff + mypy, fail-fast) → `conservation` (hard `needs:` for all downstream) → `determinism` → `unit`.** `gpu`-marked tests skipped in CI, run via `scripts/run_gpu_checks.ps1`.
   241	
   242	### 1.10 Scaffold task list & acceptance gates
   243	
   244	| # | Task | Effort | Risk | Blocks |
   245	|---|---|---|---|---|
   246	| T1 | Repo init, `.gitignore/.gitattributes`, dir tree, `__init__.py` per pkg | S | low | all |
   247	| T2 | `pyproject.toml`, pinned deps, editable install, CUDA verify | M | med | T3–T10 |
   248	| T3 | `numerics`: dtype/device, rng+manifest, `ConservedLedger`, `quat`, `solve_sym3`, `reduce` | M | med | T5,T6,T8 |
   249	| T4 | Contract stubs: `physics/contracts+force`, `fields/contracts+geology+light` | M | low | T5,T7 |
   250	| T5 | `core`: Config, SimClock, ColonyState, Colony.reset/step (no-op) | L | med | T7–T10 |
   251	| T6 | `observe`: telemetry writer + reservoir snapshot surface | M | low | T9 |
   252	| T7 | import-linter contract wired to CI | S | low | T10 |
   253	| T8 | determinism harness + `smoke_step.py` | M | med | T9 |
   254	| T9 | pytest harness, fakes, **conservation scaffold test** + determinism + boundary | M | high | T10 |
   255	| T10 | CI pipeline (conservation-first) + console smoke app | M | low | — |
   256	
   257	Sequencing: **T1 → T2 → (T3 ∥ T4) → T5 → (T6 ∥ T7 ∥ T8) → T9 → T10.**
   258	
   259	| Gate | Assertion |
   260	|---|---|
   261	| G-SCAF-1 | `pip install -e ".[dev]"` clean; cuda+cpu tensors both allocate |
   262	| G-SCAF-2 | `lint-imports` passes; an injected `physics→core` import **fails** it |
   263	| G-SCAF-3 | Closed box `closure_residual < 1e-9` over 10⁴ steps, bounded-oscillating; leak fake caught |
   264	| G-SCAF-4 | `reset(seed).step(...)` runs; all `ColonyState` shapes static; no `index_select` in step path |
   265	| G-SCAF-5 | Within-seed bit-identical on fixed device (CPU gated; CUDA reported) |
   266	| G-SCAF-6 | `smoke_step.py` prints `OK` + `bit-identical: True` on CPU and CUDA |
   267	| G-SCAF-7 | `FlatGeologyStub` substitution test: swap for a second fake `Geology` impl → no consumer line changes |
   268	
   269	Only when G-SCAF-1…7 are green does S0 begin.

   271	---
   272	
   273	## 2. S0 — SpikeSwim (the go/no-go spike)
   274	
   275	### 2.0 Purpose & the one decision S0 exists to make
   276	
   277	S0 is a **standalone batched-torch port of the donor's one-shot, frozen-heading `SwimEval.Sim.Step`** (`SwimEval.cs:740`) over `B = W·N_cap` ragged bodies, measured against four gates. It is the **go/no-go for the entire vectorization thesis** (§7.2). No ecology, no genome mutation, no steering. The frozen-heading path only: `_fThrust`/`_nThrust` constant unit vectors, `_vCom.y≡0`, no yaw integration (`StepLive` is S2, re-measured then — open Q #6).
   278	
   279	**Authorization = H1/H2 clear gate (d) while (a)(b)(c) hold** (meta-falsifier). A green **H0** number is non-conforming as authorization; any report leading with H0 is rejected.
   280	
   281	**Mass-model migration handled in two stages** (open item #3, §4.2) so "is my vectorization correct" is never entangled with "did I change the mass model":
   282	- **Stage A (oracle-clean):** generate fixtures from the donor at `ellipMassGain = 0` (box mass `max(0.1, sc.x·sc.y·sc.z·density)`), port to bit-clean match.
   283	- **Stage B (port target):** flip port segment mass to ellipsoid `(π/6)`, re-record `gain1` fixtures, re-pass gate (c). Both fixture sets retained.
   284	
   285	**Prerequisites to reading the result as authoritative:** open Q #1 (pin throughput floor to the real dev CPU/GPU — 3.07e7 is a placeholder), open Q #6 (StepLive re-measure deferred to S2), open Q #12 (`N_cap`, max-seg pinned against the H1 raggedness profile). Recorded in T17.
   286	
   287	### 2.1 Tensor layouts
   288	
   289	**Creature level `(W,N_cap)`**, flattened to `[B]` for the kernel: `alive` bool; `vcom[B,3]` (`vcom[...,1]≡0` each step); `xcom[B,3]`; `fthrust,nthrust[B,3]`; `swim_freq[B]`; `mt[B]` (tail transverse added-mass/length); `fin_active[B]` bool + `fin_S,fin_AR,fin_AL,fin_align[B]`; `mbody[B]`; `tail_gidx[B]` i64; `body_base[B]` i64.
   290	
   291	**Segment level — flattened/CSR `[S_total]`** (NOT padded-to-16, §2.4.2): `seg_localPos[S,3]`, `seg_localRot[S,4]`, `seg_abc[S,3]`, `seg_mass[S]` (box or ellipsoid per stage), `seg_areaZ[S]`, `seg_ma[S,3]`, `seg_ampDeg[S]`, `seg_phase[S]`, `seg_c[S]`, `seg_isTail[S]` bool, `seg_parent_g[S]` i64 (**global** parent = `body_base+local_parent`, or −1), `seg_depth[S]` i64, `body_id[S]` i64. Working: `pos[S,3]`, `rot[S,4]`, `prev_pos[S,3]`.
   292	
   293	**Padded ablation** `(B,16,·)+seg_mask[B,16]` — built only to *measure* the masking tax (F1), never on the main path. Every per-body reduction is `segment_index_add` over `body_id`; no boolean `index_select` in the hot loop.
   294	
   295	### 2.2 Kernel port — `Sim.Step` → batched torch (`physics/swim_step.py`)
   296	
   297	Each substep maps 1:1 to `SwimEval.cs:740–817`; all `torch.where`/mask, no Python `if` over the batch, wrapped in `torch.inference_mode()`.
   298	
   299	| # | Donor | Batched operation |
   300	|---|---|---|
   301	| S2 | `_prevPos=_pos` | `prev_pos = pos.clone()` |
   302	| S3 | `prevTip=TailTip()` | gather tail: `pos[tail_gidx] + quat_rotate(rot[tail_gidx], ẑ)*c` |
   303	| S4 | `Pose(_t)` | **6-pass depth-scan**: pass k updates `depth==k` segs by gathering parent `(pos,rot)` at `seg_parent_g`; `rot=quat_mul(pRot, localRot·angleAxis(θ,ŷ))`, `pos=pPos+quat_rotate(pRot,localPos)`; `θ_j=ampDeg_j·sin(2π·swimFreq·t+phase_j)` masked by `hasJoint`. Fixed 6 passes ⇒ static shape |
   304	| S5 | tail kinematics | `uTail=vcom+(tipNow−prevTip)/Dt`; `U=⟨uTail,fthrust⟩`, `Vt=⟨uTail,nthrust⟩`, `s=⟨quat_rotate(rot[tail],ẑ),nthrust⟩` |
   305	| S6 | reactive | `tReact=0.5·mt·(Vt²−U²s²)`; `Wt=Vt+U·s`; `pWake=0.5·mt·relu(U)·Wt²` |
   306	| S7 | fin channel | `where(fin_active, CirculatoryLiftThrust(...), 0)`→`tFin,pFin`; AoA clamp ±0.35, `q=0.5ρ·relu(U)²·S`, `CDi=0.02+CL²/(π·0.9·AR)` |
   307	| S8 | segment drag | `vloc=quat_rotate(conj(rot),uj)`; `floc=(0,0,−0.5ρ·cd·areaZ·|vloc.z|·vloc.z)`; `fDrag=seg_sum(quat_rotate(rot,floc))`, `wDrag=seg_sum(relu(−⟨fworld,uj⟩))` |
   308	| S9 | M_eff | cols `cx=quat_rotate(rot,x̂)`…; 6 entries `m00..m22 += Σ ma·(col⊗col)`; add diagonal `mbody·250` **(250 on struct only, not added-mass)** |
   309	| S10 | integrate | `fStream=(tReact+tFin)·fthrust+fDrag`; `dv=solve_sym3(m_eff, fStream·Dt)`; `vcom+=dv`; `vcom[...,1]=0`; `xcom+=vcom·Dt` |
   310	| S11 | measuring | f64 ledger accum: `ReactiveImpulse, DragImpulse, MechWork, FwdThrustImpulse` |
   311	
   312	**Build-time precompute (once/body):** reconstruct segments; LambK→`seg_ma` (f64); `mt = maX_tail/max(2c_tail,1e-4)` OR fin-rebuilt from `finMaPerp·FinReactiveScaleOf(align,gain)/(2c)` (`SwimEval.cs:575–576`); `mbody=Σ seg_mass`; `tail_gidx` by max `restPos.z`, ties → later DFS index (`SwimEval.cs:142`).
   313	
   314	`StepLedger` (f64): `p_in, t_react, p_wake, t_fin, p_fin, w_drag, dv[B,3], m_eff[B,6]`.
   315	
   316	### 2.3 Watch-items (flag, don't bulldoze)
   317	
   318	1. **KgPerSimMass=250 is asymmetric** (`SwimEval.cs:771,1023`) — scales *structural* inertia diagonal only, applied **before** adding `Σ ma·(col⊗col)`. Getting it wrong silently fails gate (c) on `dv` while `M_eff` off-diagonals match; the momentum-ledger test (T9) is the independent catch.
   319	2. **Fin-tail `mt` rebuilt from `finMaPerp`, not `maX`** — H1's ~40% fin tails exercise this in the authorizing config.
   320	3. **`_pos[j]` excludes own joint flex; `_rot[j]` includes it** (`SwimEval.cs:685–687`); `TailTip` adds `_rot[tail]·ẑ·c`. Depth-scan must preserve this asymmetry or `Vt`/`s` drift.
   321	4. **Tail tie-break = later DFS index** on equal `restPos.z` — or H1 mirror pairs pick the wrong tail.
   322	5. **Degenerate guards** (`SolveSym3` det<1e-12 fallback; `mBody≤1e-6`; empty body) must be `torch.where`, never dropped rows (breaks static shape + determinism).
   323	
   324	### 2.4 Oracle fixtures (disposition B, §7.5)
   325	
   326	`oracle/SirRobinOracle.csproj` — a Unity-light headless console (Vector3/Quaternion/Mathf shim preferred over linking `UnityEngine.dll`) driving the confirmed seams `ReconstructForTest`/`LambKForTest`/`CoastTest`/`MomentumLedger` plus a new `StepTraceForTest`. The donor is **never called live**; recorded values are checked to tolerance (§7.1.4).
   327	
   328	| Fixture | Seam | Contents | Gate |
   329	|---|---|---|---|
   330	| `lambk_grid.npz` | LambKForTest | (a,b,c) grid: sphere/2:1/4:1/10:1 prolate/oblate/degenerate → (kx,ky,kz) f64 | (c)-1 |
   331	| `reconstruct.jsonl` | ReconstructForTest | per-seg restPos/restRot/abc/mass/areaZ/ma/phase/ampDeg/isTail/parent/depth | T6/T7 |
   332	| `forces_H*.npz` | StepTraceForTest | first 32 steps of tReact,pWake,pFin,m00..m22,dv | (c)-2 |
   333	| `aggregates_H*.parquet` | Evaluate | 8s (960-step) cruiseSpeed,costOfTransport,reactiveRatio,mechWork | (c)-3 |
   334	| `coast.npz` | CoastTest | vEnd after 600 zero-force steps | invariant |
   335	| `momentum.npz` | MomentumLedger | vFinal,fHat,forwardThrustImpulse (all-sphere) | invariant |
   336	
   337	**Genome corpus** (one canonical JSON source feeds both C# and torch): **H0** homogeneous 6-seg axial; **H1** ragged 2–16, mean~6, mirror-paired, ~40% Surface fin tails; **H2** skewed (mostly 2–3 seg + rare 16). ~64 seeded genomes/class. Two configs recorded: `gain0`, `gain1`.
   338	
   339	### 2.5 The four gates (concrete tests)
   340	
   341	| Gate | Test | Assertion |
   342	|---|---|---|
   343	| **(a) Determinism** | `test_determinism.py::rerun_bit_identity` | Two same-process reruns, det-mode, CPU **and** CUDA-det: `max_abs(Δ)==0` over `(vcom,xcom,ledger)` after 960 steps. GPU-nondet: report only. |
   344	| | `::churn_deterministic` | With churn stub, rerun bit-identity still `==0` (RK-6). |
   345	| **(b) Energy inst.** | `test_energy.py::algebraic_closure` | Every step/body `|p_in−(tReact·U+pWake+pFin)|/max(|p_in|,ε) < 1e-6` (f32). |
   346	| **(b) Energy long** | `::ke_budget_1e5` | 1e5 steps `|ΔKE−Σ(P_musc−P_diss)·Dt|/Σ|work| < 1e-3` f32 / `<1e-6` f64; **gate on drift curve bounded-oscillating, not endpoint** (F6). |
   347	| **(c) LambK** | `test_lamb.py::vs_fixture` | `<1e-6` abs (f64); sphere-exact `k==0.5`; slender limits. |
   348	| **(c) Forces** | `test_step_forces.py::vs_trace` | tReact,pWake,pFin,dv,6×M_eff rel `<1e-4` (f64), first 32 steps, H1/H2. |
   349	| **(c) Aggregates** | `test_episode_oracle.py::vs_agg` | cruise/CoT/reactiveRatio rel `<1e-3`. Never gate a long-horizon bit-trace (chaotic gait, F5). |
   350	| **(d) Throughput** | `bench.py`→`test_throughput.py::floor` | creature-steps/s clears **3.07e7 with ≥10× headroom** at H1/H2 *(placeholder until Q#1)*; locate CPU↔GPU crossover `B*`; report H1/H0 + padded/flattened tax; profiler shows **force/solve-bound, not pose-scan/reduction-bound** (F2). |
   351	
   352	Ported invariants (must stay green): `test_coast_momentum.py` — zero-force coast `|vEnd−v0|<1e-4`; all-sphere `(vFinal·fHat)·M_eff == ΣtReact·Dt` within `1e-3·|impulse|+1e-6` (independent catch for the ×250 folding).
   353	
   354	### 2.6 Falsifiers
   355	
   356	| ID | Falsifier | Detector |
   357	|---|---|---|
   358	| F1 | Ragged heterogeneity defeats batching | H1 flattened slower than per-body loop, or H2 padded craters |
   359	| F2 | Depth-scan/reductions > 50% of step | profiler attribution row |
   360	| F3 | Per-op launch overhead dominates at realistic N even at r2 | rung sweep r0→r1→r2 |
   361	| F4 | Determinism tax > 2× on GPU | gate-(a) timing with/without det mode |
   362	| F5 | Oracle un-portable without de-vectorized loop | (c) aggregates diverge >1e-3 irreducibly |
   363	| F6 | f32 semi-implicit energy drift monotone | (b) long-horizon curve shape |
   364	| F7 | Churn/compaction swamps step | `churn.py` cost % of step |
   365	| **META** | **H0-green ≠ authorization** | report must lead with H1/H2 |
   366	
   367	### 2.7 Benchmark sweep (`spikeswim/bench.py`)
   368	
   369	Axes: `B ∈ {1,64,256,1024,4096,16384,65536,262144}` × `device∈{cpu,cuda}` × `dtype∈{f32,f64}` × `rung∈{r0 eager, r1 torch.compile(reduce-overhead), r2 CUDA-graph}` × `het∈{H0,H1,H2}` × `layout∈{flattened,padded}`. Protocol: warm/JIT excluded, `cuda.synchronize()` around timed region, median of K reps, `torch.profiler` on one rep/cell. One parquet row/cell + jsonl manifest (seed, config, git SHA, HW). No claim from anything but this artifact.

   397	
   398	## 3. S1–S2 — Keystone economy + canonical body
   399	
   400	Two measure-first, reversible decisions flagged inline:
   401	- **[MEASURE-1] S1 field representation** — Eulerian co-grid (default) vs Lagrangian parcels for `Bp`/`Bd`, decided by the healing-vs-consumption ratio under dense grazing (§6.2, open Q #7). This slice builds the Eulerian path + the measurement rig; parcels are the escape hatch, queued as its own slice if the fork trips.
   402	- **[MEASURE-2] `StepLive` throughput** — the yaw-integrating kernel must re-clear the S0 affordability floor **before S2 is committed** (§4.8, §7.3, open Q #6).
   403	
   404	### S1 — Conserved single-nutrient economy (the keystone)
   405	
   406	**Design inversion from the donor:** the donor grew biomass toward a static Perlin cap (`PlanktonField.cs:114`, the #1 mint bug) and discarded remineralized snow (`MarineSnowField.cs:56–58`). S1 makes both structurally impossible: every gram of N moves between tracked reservoirs, `Σ reservoirs = const` (§6.1, §6.6). The donor's *equations* (`NutrientChem.cs`) and its *conservation-test pattern* port forward; its non-conserving *architecture* does not.
   407	
   408	Closed inventory: `I_N = ΣNd + ΣBp + ΣBd + ΣBm + Σ_alive struct_N + ΣSed`. Box closed at S1 (no geology source, no burial sink) → `I_N(t)==I_N(0)` to tolerance.
   409	
   410	#### S1.A — Reservoir model + ledger (build first)
   411	
   412	| Task | Detail | Effort/Risk |
   413	|---|---|---|
   414	| **S1.1** Reservoir registry | Named f32 tensors: `Nd,Bp,Bd,Bm (W,G,G,B)`, `struct_N (W,N_cap)`, `Sed (W,G,G)` (inert at S1). Each registered with name+shape. | S/Low |
   415	| **S1.2** Paired-transfer primitive | `core/ledger.py::transfer(src,dst,amount)` debits/credits by the **identical tensor** via deterministic masked add — the ONLY sanctioned way to move N. | M/Med |
   416	| **S1.3** `close_books()` | f64 compensated sum over all registered reservoirs every N ticks; returns `closure_residual`, emits `[CONS]` line. | S/Low |
   417	
   418	```python
   419	# core/ledger.py
   420	def register_reservoir(name:str, t:Tensor)->None
   421	def transfer(src:Tensor, dst:Tensor, amount:Tensor)->None   # |Δsrc+Δdst|==0 by construction
   422	def close_books()->LedgerResidual                            # f64; asserts INV-MASS/INV-TRANSFER
   423	```
   424	
   425	**Acceptance S1.A:** random `transfer` sequence leaves `close_books().residual==0` (f64); any direct write to a registered reservoir outside `transfer` is caught by an audit test (INV-TRANSFER `<1e-6` f32/op).
   426	
   427	#### S1.B — [MEASURE-1] field representation
   428	
   429	| Task | Detail |
   430	|---|---|
   431	| **S1.4** `fields/scalar_field.py` port | Co-grid `(W,G,G,B)` with `sample(x)->(value,grad)` **trilinear interpolated** (port wrap-torus bilinear of `PlanktonField.cs:274–287`, generalized to 3D + analytic grad); transactional `deplete` returning amount-removed; f64 `total_inventory`. All biological reads via `sample` (INV-W3); graze stays on continuous position, never a cell index (P5). |
   432	| **S1.5** `core/parcels.py` stub | `Bp`/`Bd` as point-entities in the shared spatial hash. Built but **not wired** unless S1.13 trips. |
   433	| **S1.6** quantization rig | Log healing rate (diffusion+regrowth) vs consumption at graze hotspots; a forage-probe smaller than one cell must read nonzero slope from `sample.grad`. |
   434	
   435	**Decision rule (§6.2):** switch producer/detritus biomass to parcels only if dense grazing quantizes or the co-grid needs ~1 param/feature; `Nd`/temp/light stay on the grid regardless. Report the measured ratio in the S1 artifact; do not assert the default is fine.
   436	
   437	#### S1.C — The closed nutrient loop
   438	
   439	`fields/chem.py` ports `NutrientChem.cs` verbatim (batched): `monod`, `liebig_min` (S1 is `min`, not the ramp Lerp), `redfield_uptake`, `martin_weights` (sum==1).
   440	
   441	| Task | Detail |
   442	|---|---|
   443	| **S1.7** Primary production | `primary_production(Nd,Bp,light,cfg)->(dBp,dNd)`; `Gp=μ_max·min(γ_L,f_N)·Bp`; **`dNd=−dBp` exactly, `dBp←min(dBp,Nd)` strict guard, via `transfer(Nd,Bp,dBp)`. NO `baseCap`** — bloom self-terminates by emptying its own `Nd`. |
   444	| **S1.8** Remineralization + BGE split | `R=k_remin(z)·Bd`; `dBm=+BGE·R·dt` (BGE≈0.2), `dNd=+(1−BGE)·R·dt`, `dBd=−R·dt`; INVARIANT `dBm+dNd==−dBd`; two paired transfers. Fixes `MarineSnowField.cs:56` discard. |
   445	| **S1.9** Martin sinking | `Bd` sinks with `w_sink`; depth-dependent `k_remin(z)` via `martin_weights`; deposit split across bands (port `NutrientField.DepositColumn`). Detritus **accumulates** (days–weeks turnover, not donor's ~9s) so deposit-feeders get standing stock. |
   446	| **S1.10** Vertical mixing | `vertical_mix(Nd,Kz,dz,dt)` flux-form, **double-buffered** (port `NutrientField.TickTransport:46–61`); positivity clamp (INV-W5). |
   447	| **S1.11** Pump wiring | `core/economy.step()`: production→`Bd` sink→deep remin (Martin)→deep `Nd` enrichment→mixing returns it. Zonation must **emerge**; each sub-step calls only `transfer`. |
   448	
   449	Light: analytic Beer–Lambert `I(z)=I0·exp(−k_att·(−z))` (§3.9), no storage at S1.
   450	
   451	#### S1.D — Telemetry
   452	
   453	**S1.12** `[CONS]` line + inventory curve (gate on bounded-oscillating drift, not endpoint). **S1.13** Bloom/desert heatmaps of `Bp`/`Nd` + healing/consumption ratio → this is where [MEASURE-1] is read off.
   454	
   455	#### S1 Tests
   456	
   457	| Test | Asserts | Threshold |
   458	|---|---|---|
   459	| `test_transfer_paired` | `|Δsrc+Δdst|` | `<1e-6` f32 |
   460	| `test_closed_box_inventory` | draw+remin+deposit+mix leaves `I_N` invariant | `<1e-9` f64 |
   461	| `test_production_drawdown_1to1` | `dNd==−dBp`; `dBp≤Nd` | exact/guard |
   462	| `test_bge_split_closes` | `dBm+dNd==−dBd` | `<1e-9` f64 |
   463	| `test_mixing_conserves` | column Σ conserved, `≥0` | `<1e-9` f64, positivity exact |
   464	| `test_no_static_cap` | AST guard: no `baseCap`/`carryingCap`/`nicheCapFrac` drives production | symbol absent |
   465	| `test_amortized_equals_whole` | row-sliced tick == whole-grid tick | `<1e-9` f64 |
   466	| `test_soak_drift` | 1e6-step run bounded-oscillating, not monotone | drift `<1e-6` |
   467	| `test_bloom_self_terminates` | seeded pulse → bloom → drawdown → crash, no cap knob | qualitative + closed |
   468	
   469	#### S1 Acceptance Gate
   470	
   471	1. Books close `<1e-9`/step (f64), drift `<1e-6` over ≥1e6 steps, bounded-oscillating.
   472	2. Every transfer passes INV-TRANSFER `<1e-6`.
   473	3. Blooms/deserts emerge from the loop — bloom draws its own `Nd` down and crashes with **no hand-coded ceiling**; drifters plateau at production÷intake; zonation falls out of the pump.
   474	4. **[MEASURE-1] recorded** — Eulerian-vs-parcel fork decided from telemetry, number in the artifact.
   475	5. No-go honored: if the ledger cannot close `<1e-9` without an ad-hoc correction, root-cause upstream — **never re-soften depletion to re-hide a mint** (§1.2, §6.9).
   476	
   477	**S1 sequencing:** S1.1→S1.2→S1.3 (ledger first) → S1.4 → S1.7/S1.8/S1.9/S1.10 (each with its paired-transfer test green before the next) → S1.11 → S1.12/S1.13 → soak + gate. S1.5/S1.6 parallel with S1.4.
   478	
   479	### S2 — One canonical body + live locomotion
   480	
   481	**Design stance:** replace S0's hand-authored segment tensors with **genome→develop→DevelopedBody**, swap the frozen-heading kernel for the yaw-integrating live one, and **delete the scalar proxy** — morphology-through-physics is the only capability driver (§4.5, §5, §7.3). The donor laundered capability through `Measure().swimProxy` (`BodyGraph.cs:349–358`) and the `eff[]` stat vector; S2's headline gate is "kill `eff[]`."
   482	
   483	#### S2.A — Batched development scan (genome P0; dovetails S0)
   484	
   485	| Task | Detail | Effort/Risk |
   486	|---|---|---|
   487	| **S2.1** DevelopedBody contract freeze | Lock `[S_total]` SoA (§4.2): center, rest_rot, abc, mass, area, m_add, parent_idx, depth, amp_deg, phase, is_surface, is_tail, has_joint, body_id + per-body tail_index/swim_freq/swim_wave. Frozen `physics`↔`genetics` boundary. | S/Low |
   488	| **S2.2** Batched frontier unroll | `genetics/develop.py::develop(genotype)->DevelopedBody`: fixed `L=6` layers, gather→compose→scatter, precomputed unique slots (no atomics), mask-multiply never `index_select`. Reuses the S0 6-pass pose kernel shape. Port `BodyGraph.MeasureWalk:377–437` DFS **including mirror double-count (:433–435)** and the `parts≥28/depth>6` data-cap vs `16/5` physics-cap `PropWalk (:442–460)`. | L/High |
   489	| **S2.3** Aggregate hard-diff | Emit donor `Measure()` aggregates from torch: `{mass,area,intake,bulk,avgDensity,parts}` + `{length,girth,fineness,propArea,asymmetry,compactness}` (`BodyGraph.cs:314–375`). | M/Med |
   490	
   491	**P0 acceptance:** torch develop reproduces C# `Measure()` aggregates within **rel 1e-4 (f64)** on an H1 sample, preserving traversal order + mirror double-count, *before* any new capability is layered on.
   492	
   493	#### S2.B — Tree→graph + ellipsoid readout (genome P1)
   494	
   495	| Task | Detail | Effort/Risk |
   496	|---|---|---|
   497	| **S2.4** Genotype SoA + innovation ids | `genetics/genotype.py`: `PartGene` tree (`BodyGraph.cs:23`)→node/edge tables; children→edges; `mirror`/`recursion_r` as edge fields; monotone `next_iid` (`genetics/innovation.py`). Still asexual (crossover is S5). | L/Med |
   498	| **S2.5** Box→ellipsoid `(a,b,c)` | Replace box `size` with ellipsoid semi-axes; **ellipsoid volume `(π/6)·2a·2b·2c` for BOTH inertia and displacement** (§4.2 migration note). Log-scale `log_a/log_b/log_c` to kill the additive ratchet (§5.2). | Med-High |
   499	
   500	**P1 acceptance:** develop still matches P0; **ellipsoid readout re-validated vs box baseline, and SwimEval oracle fixtures (LambK grid, single-step forces) re-recorded** if the a/b/c reinterpretation breaches `<1e-4` rel (§4.8, RK-11). This is the `gain1` re-baseline flagged in S0/T11.
   501	
   502	#### S2.C — [MEASURE-2] StepLive + throughput re-measurement
   503	
   504	| Task | Detail | Effort/Risk |
   505	|---|---|---|
   506	| **S2.6** Additive-contributor core | Stand up `ForceContributor` (§4.5) with `F_hydro` (ported SwimEval) as the **sole** contributor. Core owns articulated state, `M_eff` assembly, `solve_sym3` semi-implicit integrator, energy ledger. Gravity/buoyancy/contact NOT built now — but the summation seam is (avoids the donor's swimming-only dead-end). | M/Med |
   507	| **S2.7** `step_live.py` (yaw-integrating) | Extend S0 `Step` (§4.4): latched DC bias `θ_j += turnCmd·depth_j`; real yaw torque `τ=Σ r×F` integrated as **angular momentum** `L_yaw+=τ·Dt`, `ω=L_yaw/I_yaw`, quadratic yaw drag `τ_drag=−Cyaw·ω·|ω|`. `turnCmd` = P-controller on heading error (the one "placeholder brain" seam), bounded within `AmpMax=58°`. Realized DOF exactly `{surge,yaw}` (validates CORE contract, §7.4.2). | L/Med |
   508	| **S2.8** Throughput re-measure | Re-run the S0 sweep on `StepLive` (heavier: yaw state + P-controller + steering torque). | M/High |
   509	
   510	**[MEASURE-2] rule:** `StepLive` at H1/H2 must re-clear the floor (3.07e7 ×10 headroom, or the Q#1-pinned number) with the profiler force/solve-bound. **If it fails, stop and re-scope** (narrow GPU to many-worlds / keep S2 on CPU via `device=`, RK-2) — do not bulldoze a slower kernel.
   511	
   512	#### S2.D — Kill `eff[]`; derive capability from morphology (P2)
   513	
   514	The `eff[]` stat vector and `swimProxy` scalar are **deleted, not migrated** (§2.5, §5.4, §7.3).
   515	
   516	| Task | Capability | Derived from | Effort |
   517	|---|---|---|---|

   520	| **S2.11** Mass/buoyancy | sinking, COT, neutral-buoyancy niche | `Σ density·volume` + displaced ellipsoid volume | S |
   521	| **S2.12** Defense | armored vs fast | bulk/density vs agility(compactness+streamlining) — derived, not a slot | M |
   522	| **S2.13** `eff[]`/`swimProxy` deletion + guard | remove the proxy path; AST guard test | grep: no `eff`/`swimProxy`/`speed`-scalar drives feeding/loco/defense | S |
   523	
   524	Metabolism (Kleiber `P_basal=B0·M^α`, §6.5.1) wired here as a read on derived mass, feeding S1 energy accounting; full feeding economy is S3.
